. Moreover,
because
\mathbf{PSh}(-)
preserves strict equalities, this gives rise to a 2-functor

\mathcal{M}\times\Delta^{1}
\mathbf{Cat}
. As a morphism defined by precomposition each
\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*}
has a
left adjoint and a right adjoint:
\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}_{!}\dashv\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*}\dashv\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}_{*}
. The left
adjoint extends
\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}
in the sense that
\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}_{!}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbf{y}(\Theta)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbf{y}(\Theta)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbf{y}(\Theta)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{y}(\Theta)$}\mathclose{}{)}}\cong\mathbf{y}(\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}(\Theta))
. We
may now apply
Theorem

4.13
.

Theorem 6.1
.

There exist a model
\mathcal{G}\llbracket-\rrbracket
of
MTT
with a hierarchy of cumulative universes in

\mathbf{Gl}(\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*})
.

In this model,
\mathcal{G}\llbracket m\rrbracket
is
\mathbf{Gl}(\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*})
, and
\mathcal{G}\llbracket\mu\rrbracket
is interpreted by
precomposition with
\mu
in both components. Furthermore, there is a (dependent) open and closed
modality in
\mathcal{G}\llbracket m\rrbracket
, preserved by all modalities.

In what follows, we will freely use
MTT
to work with this model. As the interpretation of
identity types in this model supports equality reflection, we will work with extensional equality
when reasoning with
MTT
.

6.2.
Basic properties of
\mathcal{G}\llbracket m\rrbracket

The
MTT
modalities in this model are particularly well-behaved because the arise from the inverse
image of essential geometric morphisms. In particular, they preserve colimits internally to the
theory.

Theorem 6.2
.

For each
\mu
there is an equivalence
\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A+B$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A+B$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A+B$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A+B$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}\simeq\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}+\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid B$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid B$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid B$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid B$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}
.

Proof.

Recall that each modality in this model is interpreted by a morphism which is simultaneously a
left and right adjoint. Therefore, this model can be extended to the mode theory
\mathcal{M}_{\mathsf{adj}}
, which
extends
\mathcal{M}
by adding a right adjoint
\bar{\mu}
for each
\mu
from

\mathcal{M}

[
Gra+20
, Section 10.2]
.

The
crisp induction
principles derived in
[
Gra+20
, Section 10.5]
now
show that
\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid-$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid-$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid-$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid-$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}
preserves coproducts internally with this extended mode theory, so the
equivalence is validated by the model from
Theorem

6.1
(though is no longer internally
derivable).
∎

Remark 8
.

We note that this model of
MTT
enjoys

\Gamma.(\mu\circ\nu\mid A)\cong\Gamma.(\mu\mid\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}})
: the modalities are interpreted
by proper dependent right adjoints. We therefore blur the distinction between a variable

\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid x:A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid x:A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid x:A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid x:A$}\mathclose{}{)}}
and a variable
\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{id}\mid x:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{id}\mid x:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{id}\mid x:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{id}\mid x:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
.

Notation 6.3
.

It’s convenient to recall that
\fullmoon
is defined by exponentiation with a subterminal (a
proposition)
\mathbf{syn}
. We accordingly write
\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z:\mathbf{syn}}A(z)
.

Remark 9
.

Strictly speaking,
\mathbf{syn}
should contain a mode annotation. However,

\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\mathbf{syn}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\mathbf{syn}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\mathbf{syn}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\mathbf{syn}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}\cong\mathbf{syn}_{m}
, and as both are subterminal we identify
\mathbf{syn}_{m}
and

\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\mathbf{syn}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\mathbf{syn}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\mathbf{syn}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\mathbf{syn}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}
.

Finally, we note also that
\fullmoon
and
\newmoon
enjoy two important properties:

Theorem 6.4

(Fracture
[
AGV72
]
)
.

For any type
A
we have
A\cong\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}A\times_{\textnormal{$\newmoon$}\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}A}\textnormal{$\newmoon$}A
.

Remark 10
.

The following constructive proof is due to Christian Sattler.

. Under this presentation moreover,
\mathbf{syn}
can be defined as
follows:

\mathbf{syn}(\Theta)=\emptyset\qquad\mathbf{syn}(\Gamma)=\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\star$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\star$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\star$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\star$}\mathclose{}{\}}}

The levelwise decidability of
\mathbf{syn}
is then immediate.
∎

In anticipation of using this model of
MTT
to construct the normalization model in the next
section, we also introduce several constants interpreted by specific presheaves in
\mathcal{G}\llbracket m\rrbracket
.

Definition 6.8
.

There is pair of types

in
\mathcal{G}\llbracket m\rrbracket
given by normal and neutral forms viewed as a presheaf over

\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
. Similarly, there is a closed type
\mathsf{NfTy}_{m}
of normal types lying over

\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathcal{T}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{T}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{T}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
.

Definition 6.9
.

There is a type
\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{m}(z)\vdash\mathsf{V}_{m}
given by viewing the presheaf of variables as
lying over
\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
. There is an induced inclusion
\mathsf{V}_{m}(A)
\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(A)

which we shall treat as silent.

Using realignment if necessary, we choose
\mathsf{Nf}_{m}
,
\mathsf{Ne}_{m}
,
\mathsf{V}_{m}
such that under the
assumptions
z:\mathbf{syn}
and
A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{m}(z)
, we have the following equations:

\mathsf{V}_{m}(A)=\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(A)=\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(A)=\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(z,A(z))

Similarly, under just the assumption of
z
, we have
\mathsf{NfTy}_{m}=\mathsf{Ty}_{m}(z)
.

6.3.
An
MTT
cosmos, internally

In
Section

7
we will construct a model of
MTT
in
\mathcal{G}\llbracket-\rrbracket
lying over
the syntactic model. To that end, we recast the existence of a model of
MTT
into a structure we
can express in the internal language of
\mathcal{G}\llbracket-\rrbracket
. Explicitly, we rephrase

Definition

3.3
as a sequence of constants in the internal type theory e.g., a pair

\mathsf{Ty}_{m}
and
\mathsf{Ty}_{m}\vdash\mathsf{Tm}_{m}
, combinators for all the type codes, etc. The full list of
constants is described in
Appendix

B
.

Remark 11
.

There is potential for confusion here: we are using the internal language of
\mathcal{G}\llbracket-\rrbracket

(extensional
MTT
) as a framework to express the structure of an
MTT
cosmos. This is not a
circularity; the interpretation of extensional
MTT
into
\mathcal{G}\llbracket-\rrbracket
is formulated in terms
of the models and metatheory developed already in
[
Gra+20
]
.

Already, we can immediately obtain an
MTT
cosmos internal to
\mathcal{G}\llbracket-\rrbracket
by restricting to the
open subtopos:
\mathcal{S}\llbracket-\rrbracket
. In this cosmos, types are interpreted as elements of the constant

\mathsf{Ty}_{m}(z)
while terms are elements of
\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(z,-)
. This the
syntactic

MTT

cosmos. Externally, this is the
MTT
cosmos
\mathcal{S}\llbracket-\rrbracket
where universes are interpreted by the
traditional representable natural transformations
[
Awo18
]
. Eventually, we will
construct the normalization model as a second cosmos in
\mathcal{G}\llbracket-\rrbracket
which lies strictly over the
syntactic cosmos.

We will use the “unqualified” names such as
\mathsf{Prod}(z)
to refer to elements of this syntactic
cosmos. So, for instance, under the assumption
z:\mathbf{syn}
we the following:

\displaystyle\mathsf{Mod}[\mu]

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\mathsf{Ty}_{m}(z)

\displaystyle\mathsf{m}[\mu]

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid a:\mathsf{Tm}_{n}(z,A)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid a:\mathsf{Tm}_{n}(z,A)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid a:\mathsf{Tm}_{n}(z,A)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid a:\mathsf{Tm}_{n}(z,A)$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](A))

\displaystyle\mathsf{letmod}[\mu;\nu]

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\circ\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\circ\mu\mid Similarly, we will abusively write
\mathsf{Prod}(A,B)
for
\lambda z.\ \mathsf{Prod}(A(z),B(-,z))
.

6.4.
Higher-order abstract syntax for neutral and normal forms

The types
\mathsf{Ne}_{m}
,
\mathsf{Nf}_{m}
can be used to encode a form of HOAS inside of

\mathcal{G}\llbracket m\rrbracket

[
Hof99
]
. We record the constants that result from this in

Appendix

C
.

In the proof of normalization we will take advantage of the fact that under an open modality, a
normal and neutral form decode to the appropriate terms. Therefore, under the open modality

\mathbf{proj}_{0}(\mathbf{pair}(M,N))
is well-typed and equal to
M
along with other expected equations.

7.
The normalization model

We now construct a model of
MTT
in
\mathcal{G}\llbracket-\rrbracket
which lies over the over the syntactic model of

MTT
built around
\mathsf{Ty}_{m}
and
\mathsf{Tm}_{m}
introduced in

Section

6.3
. Concretely, this means that we must construct a series of
constants (
\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}
,
\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}
,
\mathsf{Prod}^{*}
, etc.) in
\mathcal{G}\llbracket-\rrbracket
such that under
z:\mathbf{syn}

these constants are equal to their corresponding syntactic components (
\mathsf{Ty}_{m}(z)
,
\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(z,-)
,

\mathsf{Prod}(z)
, etc.). This family of constants will define a model in
\mathcal{G}\llbracket-\rrbracket
and the
‘alignment’ condition ensures that there is a morphism of models
(
Theorem

7.9
). See
Appendix

B
for the full list of
constants. Throughout this section we will use extensional
MTT
as an internal language for

\mathcal{G}\llbracket-\rrbracket
(
Theorem

6.1
) to define these constants. The strict equations will follow
from repeated applications of the realignment theorem (
Theorem

6.5
). Many of
these computations will be familiar to readers experienced with STC. The main novelties are modal
connectives
Lemmas

7.5
and
7.6
.

Definition 7.1
.

We define
\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}
as the realignment of the following over
\mathsf{Ty}_{m}
:

\qquad\begin{array}[t]{l}{\textbf{record}}\ T:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{U}_{2}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\displaystyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Ty}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{U}_{2}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\textstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Ty}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{U}_{2}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Ty}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{U}_{2}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Ty}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}\ {\textbf{where}}\\
\ \begin{array}[t]{l}\mathfrak{A}:\mathsf{NfTy}_{m}\\
\Phi:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{U}_{1}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\displaystyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(z,\mathfrak{A})$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{U}_{1}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\textstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(z,\mathfrak{A})$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{U}_{1}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(z,\mathfrak{A})$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{U}_{1}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(z,\mathfrak{A})$}\mathclose{}{\}}}\\
{\uparrow}:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}\mathfrak{A})\to\Phi$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\displaystyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{id}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}\mathfrak{A})\to\Phi$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\textstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{id}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}\mathfrak{A})\to\Phi$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{id}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}\mathfrak{A})\to\Phi$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{id}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}\\
{\downarrow}:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\Phi\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}\mathfrak{A})$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\displaystyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{id}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\Phi\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}\mathfrak{A})$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\textstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{id}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Phi\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}\mathfrak{A})$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{id}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Phi\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}\mathfrak{A})$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{id}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}\end{array}\end{array}

Taking advantage of the isomorphism
\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}\cong T
, we construct elements of
\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}
by
specifying
\mathfrak{A}
,
\Phi
,
{\uparrow}
, and
{\downarrow}
. By realignment,

\eta(\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathfrak{A};\Phi;{\downarrow};{\uparrow}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathfrak{A};\Phi;{\downarrow};{\uparrow}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathfrak{A};\Phi;{\downarrow};{\uparrow}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathfrak{A};\Phi;{\downarrow};{\uparrow}$}\mathclose{}{\}}})=\eta(\mathfrak{A})
.

Definition 7.2
.

We define
A:\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}\vdash\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(A){\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}{m}}
by
\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\_;\Phi;\_;\_$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\_;\Phi;\_;\_$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\_;\Phi;\_;\_$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\_;\Phi;\_;\_$}\mathclose{}{\}}})=\Phi
.

Lemma 7.3
.

\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}
lies strictly over
\mathsf{Tm}_{m}
. Explicitly for each
A:\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}=\mathsf{Ty}_{m}(z)
,

\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(A)=\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(z,A)
.

Proof.

Using the boundary condition on
\Phi
, we compute

\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(A)=\Phi_{A}=\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(z,\mathfrak{A}_{A})
. Under the hypothesis
z:\mathbf{syn}
, we obtain an
equality
A=\mathfrak{A}_{A}
because
\eta(A)=\eta(\mathfrak{A}_{A})
by definition. The conclusion now
follows.
∎

Lemma 7.4
.

Fixing
T_{0}:\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}
and
T_{1}:\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(T_{0})\to\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}
, there exists a pair of constants:

\displaystyle\mathsf{Sig}^{*}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\displaystyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Sig}(z,T_{0},T_{1})$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\textstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Sig}(z,T_{0},T_{1})$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Sig}(z,T_{0},T_{1})$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Sig}(z,T_{0},T_{1})$}\mathclose{}{\}}}

\displaystyle\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}^{*}}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big\{}{\Big\{}{\big\{}{\{}}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Sig}^{*}(T_{0},T_{1}))\cong\textstyle\sum_{t:\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(T_{0})}\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(T_{1}(t))$}\mathrel{{\mathchoice{\Big|}{\Big|}{\big|}{|}}}\hbox{$\displaystyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}}(z,T_{0},T_{1})$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big\}}{\Big\}}{\big\}}{\}}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big\{}{\Big\{}{\big\{}{\{}}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Sig}^{*}(T_{0},T_{1}))\cong\textstyle\sum_{t:\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(T_{0})}\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(T_{1}(t))$}\mathrel{{\mathchoice{\Big|}{\Big|}{\big|}{|}}}\hbox{$\textstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}}(z,T_{0},T_{1})$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big\}}{\Big\}}{\big\}}{\}}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big\{}{\Big\{}{\big\{}{\{}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Sig}^{*}(T_{0},T_{1}))\cong\textstyle\sum_{t:\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(T_{0})}\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(T_{1}(t))$}\mathrel{{\mathchoice{\Big|}{\Big|}{\big|}{|}}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}}(z,T_{0},T_{1})$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big\}}{\Big\}}{\big\}}{\}}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big\{}{\Big\{}{\big\{}{\{}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Sig}^{*}(T_{0},T_{1}))\cong\textstyle\sum_{t:\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(T_{0})}\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(T_{1}(t))$}\mathrel{{\mathchoice{\Big|}{\Big|}{\big|}{|}}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}}(z,T_{0},T_{1})$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big\}}{\Big\}}{\big\}}{\}}}}

Proof.

Let us start by apply
Theorem

6.5
to
\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}(z)}
and

\textstyle\sum_{t_{0}:\Phi_{T_{0}}}\Phi_{T_{1}(t_{0})}
to produce
\Psi:\mathsf{U}_{1}
such that

z:\mathbf{syn}\vdash\Psi=\mathsf{Sig}(z,T_{0},T_{1})
and

\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}^{*}}:\Psi\cong\textstyle\sum_{t_{0}:\Phi_{T_{0}}}\Phi_{T_{1}(t_{0})}
which restricts to

\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}}
under
z:\mathbf{syn}
.

We now define
\mathsf{Sig}^{*}(T_{0},T_{1})
as follows:

\displaystyle\mathfrak{A}_{\mathsf{Sig}^{*}(T_{0},T_{1})}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{Sum}}(\mathfrak{A}_{T_{0}},\lambda v.\ \mathfrak{A}_{T_{1}({\uparrow}_{T_{0}}v)})

\displaystyle\Phi_{\mathsf{Sig}^{*}(T_{0},T_{1})}

\displaystyle=\Psi

\displaystyle{\uparrow}_{\mathsf{Sig}^{*}(T_{0},T_{1})}

\displaystyle=\lambda e.\ \alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}^{*}}^{-1}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\uparrow}_{T_{0}}(\mathbf{proj}_{0}(e)),{\uparrow}_{T_{1}({\uparrow}_{T_{0}}(\mathbf{proj}_{0}(e)))}(\mathbf{proj}_{1}(e))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle{\uparrow}_{T_{0}}(\mathbf{proj}_{0}(e)),{\uparrow}_{T_{1}({\uparrow}_{T_{0}}(\mathbf{proj}_{0}(e)))}(\mathbf{proj}_{1}(e))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\uparrow}_{T_{0}}(\mathbf{proj}_{0}(e)),{\uparrow}_{T_{1}({\uparrow}_{T_{0}}(\mathbf{proj}_{0}(e)))}(\mathbf{proj}_{1}(e))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\uparrow}_{T_{0}}(\mathbf{proj}_{0}(e)),{\uparrow}_{T_{1}({\uparrow}_{T_{0}}(\mathbf{proj}_{0}(e)))}(\mathbf{proj}_{1}(e))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}

\displaystyle{\downarrow}_{\mathsf{Sig}^{*}(T_{0},T_{1})}

\displaystyle=\lambda t.\ \mathbf{pair}({\downarrow}_{T_{0}}(\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}^{*}}(t)_{0}),{\downarrow}_{T_{1}(\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}^{*}}(t)_{0})}(\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}^{*}}(t)_{1}))

The fact that
{\downarrow}
and
{\uparrow}
lie over the identity follows directly from the
\beta

and
\eta
laws of dependent sums in
MTT
. We show the calculations for
{\uparrow}
. Fix
z:\mathbf{syn}
:

\displaystyle{\uparrow}_{\mathsf{Sig}^{*}(T_{0},T_{1})}(e)

\displaystyle=\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}^{*}}^{-1}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\uparrow}_{T_{0}}(\mathbf{proj}_{0}(e)),{\uparrow}_{T_{1}({\uparrow}_{T_{0}}(\mathbf{proj}_{0}(e)))}(\mathbf{proj}_{1}(e))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle{\uparrow}_{T_{0}}(\mathbf{proj}_{0}(e)),{\uparrow}_{T_{1}({\uparrow}_{T_{0}}(\mathbf{proj}_{0}(e)))}(\mathbf{proj}_{1}(e))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\uparrow}_{T_{0}}(\mathbf{proj}_{0}(e)),{\uparrow}_{T_{1}({\uparrow}_{T_{0}}(\mathbf{proj}_{0}(e)))}(\mathbf{proj}_{1}(e))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\uparrow}_{T_{0}}(\mathbf{proj}_{0}(e)),{\uparrow}_{T_{1}({\uparrow}_{T_{0}}(\mathbf{proj}_{0}(e)))}(\mathbf{proj}_{1}(e))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}}^{-1}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbf{proj}_{0}(e),\mathbf{proj}_{1}(e)$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbf{proj}_{0}(e),\mathbf{proj}_{1}(e)$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbf{proj}_{0}(e),\mathbf{proj}_{1}(e)$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{proj}_{0}(e),\mathbf{proj}_{1}(e)$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}}^{-1}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}(T_{0},T_{1})}(e)_{0},\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}(T_{0},T_{1})}(e)_{1}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}(T_{0},T_{1})}(e)_{0},\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}(T_{0},T_{1})}(e)_{1}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}(T_{0},T_{1})}(e)_{0},\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}(T_{0},T_{1})}(e)_{1}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}(T_{0},T_{1})}(e)_{0},\alpha_{\mathsf{Sig}(T_{0},T_{1})}(e)_{1}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}

\displaystyle=e

The fact that
\mathfrak{A}_{\mathsf{Sig}(T_{0},T_{1})}
and

\Phi_{\mathsf{Sig}^{*}(T_{0},T_{1})}
lie over
\mathsf{Sig}(T_{0},T_{1})
and
\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(z,\mathsf{Sig}(z,T_{0},T_{1}))

respectively follows from definition and realignment.
∎

Lemma 7.5
.

Fixing
T_{0}:(\mu\mid\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{n})
and
T_{1}:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(T_{0})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(T_{0})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(T_{0})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(T_{0})$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}
, there exists a pair of constants:

\displaystyle\mathsf{Prod}^{*}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{Ty}_{m}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\displaystyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Prod}^{*}(z,T_{0},T_{1})$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{Ty}_{m}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\textstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Prod}^{*}(z,T_{0},T_{1})$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{Ty}_{m}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Prod}^{*}(z,T_{0},T_{1})$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{Ty}_{m}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Prod}^{*}(z,T_{0},T_{1})$}\mathclose{}{\}}}

\displaystyle\alpha_{\mathsf{Prod}^{*}}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big\{}{\Big\{}{\big\{}{\{}}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Prod}(T_{0},T_{1}))\cong\textstyle\prod_{t:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(T_{0})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(T_{0})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(T_{0})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(T_{0})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}}\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(T_{1}(t))$}\mathrel{{\mathchoice{\Big|}{\Big|}{\big|}{|}}}\hbox{$\displaystyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\alpha_{\mathsf{Prod}}(z,T_{0},T_{1})$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big\}}{\Big\}}{\big\}}{\}}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big\{}{\Big\{}{\big\{}{\{}}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Prod}(T_{0},T_{1}))\cong\textstyle\prod_{t:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(T_{0})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(T_{0})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(T_{0})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(T_{0})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}}\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(T_{1}(t))$}\mathrel{{\mathchoice{\Big|}{\Big|}{\big|}{|}}}\hbox{$\textstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\alpha_{\mathsf{Prod}}(z,T_{0},T_{1})$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big\}}{\Big\}}{\big\}}{\}}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big\{}{\Big\{}{\big\{}{\{}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Prod}(T_{0},T_{1}))\cong\textstyle\prod_{t:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(T_{0})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(T_{0})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(T_{0})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(T_{0})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}}\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(T_{1}(t))$}\mathrel{{\mathchoice{\Big|}{\Big|}{\big|}{|}}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\alpha_{\mathsf{Prod}}(z,T_{0},T_{1})$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big\}}{\Big\}}{\big\}}{\}}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big\{}{\Big\{}{\big\{}{\{}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Prod}(T_{0},T_{1}))\cong\textstyle\prod_{t:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(T_{0})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(T_{0})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(T_{0})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(T_{0})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}}\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(T_{1}(t))$}\mathrel{{\mathchoice{\Big|}{\Big|}{\big|}{|}}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\alpha_{\mathsf{Prod}}(z,T_{0},T_{1})$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big\}}{\Big\}}{\big\}}{\}}}}

Proof.

Apply
Theorem

6.5
to
\alpha_{\mathsf{Prod}(z)}
and

\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid t_{0}:\Phi_{T_{0}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid t_{0}:\Phi_{T_{0}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid t_{0}:\Phi_{T_{0}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid t_{0}:\Phi_{T_{0}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\Phi_{T_{1}(t_{0})}
to produce
\Psi:\mathsf{U}_{1}
such that

z:\mathbf{syn}\vdash\Psi=\mathsf{Prod}(z,T_{0},T_{1})
and

\alpha_{\mathsf{Prod}^{*}}:\Psi\cong\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid t_{0}:\Phi_{T_{0}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid t_{0}:\Phi_{T_{0}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid t_{0}:\Phi_{T_{0}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid t_{0}:\Phi_{T_{0}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\Phi_{T_{1}(t_{0})}
which restricts to

\alpha_{\mathsf{Prod}}
under
z:\mathbf{syn}
. We now define
\mathsf{Prod}(T_{0},T_{1})
as follows:

\displaystyle\mathfrak{A}_{\mathsf{Prod}^{*}(T_{0},T_{1})}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{Prod}}(\mathfrak{A}_{T_{0}},\lambda v.\ \mathfrak{A}_{T_{1}({\uparrow}_{T_{0}}v)})

\displaystyle\Phi_{\mathsf{Prod}^{*}(T_{0},T_{1})}

\displaystyle=\Psi

\displaystyle{\uparrow}_{\mathsf{Prod}^{*}(T_{0},T_{1})}

\displaystyle=\lambda e.\ \alpha_{\mathsf{Prod}}^{-1}(\lambda t.\ {\uparrow}_{T_{1}(t)}(\mathbf{app}(e)({\downarrow}_{T_{0}}(t))))

\displaystyle{\downarrow}_{\mathsf{Prod}^{*}(T_{0},T_{1})}

\displaystyle=\lambda t.\ \mathbf{lam}(\lambda v.\ {\uparrow}_{T_{1}({\uparrow}_{T_{0}}(v))}(\alpha_{\mathsf{Prod}}(t)({\uparrow}_{T_{0}}(v))))

Once again, the fact that everything lies over the correct terms follows from the
\beta
and

\eta
laws for dependent products, as well as the conclusions of realignment.
∎

Proof.

As is typical now, we will proceed by realigment. We define
\Phi
to be the realignment of the
following type along the subsequent isomorphism:

\displaystyle\Psi

\displaystyle=\textstyle\sum_{m:\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))}\textnormal{$\newmoon$}\mathchoice{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big[}{\Big[}{\big[}{[}}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\textstyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}+\mathchoice{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\sum_{a:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}}\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m)$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\textstyle\textstyle\sum_{a:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}}\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m)$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{a:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}}\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m)$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{a:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}}\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m)$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big]}{\Big]}{\big]}{]}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big[}{\Big[}{\big[}{[}}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\textstyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}+\mathchoice{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\sum_{a:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox hopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}}\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m)$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\textstyle\textstyle\sum_{a:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}}\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m)$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{a:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}}\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m)$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{a:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}}\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m)$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big]}{\Big]}{\big]}{]}}}

\displaystyle\alpha_{\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}}

\displaystyle:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}\Psi\cong\textstyle\sum_{m:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))}\mathbf{1}\cong\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(z,\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](z,T))

This gives a type
\Phi
and an isomorphism
\alpha:\Phi\cong\Psi
such that

\alpha^{-1}(\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle m,\dots$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle m,\dots$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m,\dots$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m,\dots$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}})=m
in a context with
z:\mathbf{syn}
.

We now use this to define
\mathsf{Mod}^{*}[\mu](T)
:

\displaystyle\mathfrak{A}_{\mathsf{Mod}^{*}[\mu](T)}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{Mod}_{\mu}(\mathfrak{A}_{T})

\displaystyle\Phi_{\mathsf{Mod}^{*}[\mu](T)}

\displaystyle=\Phi

\displaystyle{\uparrow}_{\mathsf{Mod}^{*}[\mu](T)}

\displaystyle=\lambda e.\alpha^{-1}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbf{up}(e),\eta(\mathsf{in}_{0}(\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle e,\star$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle e,\star$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle e,\star$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle e,\star$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbf{up}(e),\eta(\mathsf{in}_{0}(\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle e,\star$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle e,\star$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle e,\star$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle e,\star$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbf{up}(e),\eta(\mathsf{in}_{0}(\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle e,\star$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle e,\star$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle e,\star$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle e,\star$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{up}(e),\eta(\mathsf{in}_{0}(\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle e,\star$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle e,\star$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle e,\star$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle e,\star$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}

\displaystyle{\downarrow}_{\mathsf{Mod}^{*}[\mu](T)}

\displaystyle=\pi_{0}\circ\alpha

Unlike with dependent sums and products, however, this is not the end of the story. We must also
define the introduction and elimination forms for this type. First, the intro form:

\mathsf{m}^{*}[\mu](T,m)=\alpha^{-1}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}(m)),\eta(\mathsf{in}_{1}(m,\star))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}(m)),\eta(\mathsf{in}_{1}(m,\star))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}(m)),\eta(\mathsf{in}_{1}(m,\star))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}(m)),\eta(\mathsf{in}_{1}(m,\star))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}

In order to define the elimination form, we need the elimination principle for
\textnormal{$\newmoon$}A
. Recall

\textnormal{$\newmoon$}A=\mathbf{syn}\mathbin{\star}A
so we may use the induction principle of a pushout on
\textnormal{$\newmoon$}A
.

With this in mind, we define the elimination rule
\mathsf{letmod}^{*}[\mu;\nu](T,T_{m},b,s)
as
follows. First, recall that
s:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}^{*}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}^{*}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}^{*}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}^{*}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}
. We use the fact that

\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\mid-$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\mid-$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\mid-$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\mid-$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}
preserves dependent sums, the closed modality (
Theorem

6.1
), and
coproducts (
Theorem

6.2
) to decompose
s
into two terms:

\displaystyle m

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}

\displaystyle q

\displaystyle:\textnormal{$\newmoon$}\mathchoice{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big[}{\Big[}{\big[}{[}}\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\mid\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}+\textstyle\sum_{a:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\circ\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\circ\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\circ\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\circ\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\mid\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}})$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big]}{\Big]}{\big]}{]}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big[}{\Big[}{\big[}{[}}\hbox{$\textstyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\mid\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}+\textstyle\sum_{a:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\circ\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\circ\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\circ\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\circ\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\mid\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}})$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big]}{\Big]}{\big]}{]}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big[}{\Big[}{\big[}{[}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\mid\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}+\textstyle\sum_{a:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\circ\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\circ\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\circ\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\circ\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\mid\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}})$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big]}{\Big]}{\big]}{]}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big[}{\Big[}{\big[}{[}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\mid\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](T))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\mid\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}+\textstyle\sum_{a:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\circ\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\circ\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\circ\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\circ\mu\mid\Phi_{T}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\mid\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\mid\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}({\downarrow}_{T}a)=m$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}})$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big]}{\Big]}{\big]}{]}}}

We may now perform induction on
q
:

\begin{cases}\mathsf{letmod}[\mu;\nu](z,T,T_{m},b,s)&q=\mathsf{in}_{0}(z)\\
{\downarrow}_{T_{m}(s)}\ \mathbf{letmod}_{\mu;\nu}(T,T_{m},\lambda x.\ {\downarrow}_{T_{m}(\mathsf{m}[\mu]({\uparrow}_{T}x))}b({\uparrow}_{T}x),e)&q=\mathsf{in}_{1}(\mathsf{in}_{0}(\mathsf{mod}_{\nu}(e),\_))\\
b(a)&q=\mathsf{in}_{1}(\mathsf{in}_{1}(\mathsf{mod}_{\nu}(a),\_))\end{cases}

The fact that these agree on overlaps follows from the
\beta
rule for modal types from

MTT
. By construction, the elimination and introduction forms lie over their syntactic
counterparts, and calculation shows that the
\beta
equation holds.
∎

Lemma 7.7
.

The following constants exist:

\displaystyle\mathsf{Bool}^{*}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\displaystyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Bool}(z)$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\textstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Bool}(z)$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Bool}(z)$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Bool}(z)$}\mathclose{}{\}}}

\displaystyle\mathsf{true}^{*}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\displaystyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{true}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\textstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{true}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{true}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{true}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}

\displaystyle\mathsf{false}^{*}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\displaystyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{false}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\textstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{false}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{false}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{false}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}

\displaystyle\mathsf{if}^{*}

\displaystyle:(T:\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool}(z))\to\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m})\to

\displaystyle\qquad\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(T(\mathsf{true}^{*}))\to\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(T(\mathsf{false}^{*}))\to(b:\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool}^{*}))\to

\displaystyle\qquad\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(T(b))\mid z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{if}(T,t,f,b)$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(T(b))\mid z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{if}(T,t,f,b)$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(T(b))\mid z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{if}(T,t,f,b)$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(T(b))\mid z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{if}(T,t,f,b)$}\mathclose{}{\}}}

\displaystyle\_

\displaystyle:(T:\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool}^{*})\to\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m})(t:\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(T(\mathsf{true}^{*})))(f:\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(T(\mathsf{false}^{*})))\to

\displaystyle\qquad(\mathsf{if}^{*}(T,t,f,\mathsf{true}^{*})=t)\times(\mathsf{if}^{*}(T,t,f,\mathsf{false}^{*})=f)

Proof.

The proof proceeds much as
Lemma

7.6
, so we present the relevant definitions
without much commentary. First, we realign
\Psi
along
\alpha_{\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}}
:

\displaystyle\Psi

\displaystyle=\textstyle\sum_{m:\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool}^{*})}\textnormal{$\newmoon$}\mathchoice{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big[}{\Big[}{\big[}{[}}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\textstyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}+\mathchoice{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\sum_{b:\mathbf{2}}m=\mathsf{rec}_{\mathbf{2}}(b;\mathsf{tt};\mathsf{ff}))$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\textstyle\textstyle\sum_{b:\mathbf{2}}m=\mathsf{rec}_{\mathbf{2}}(b;\mathsf{tt};\mathsf{ff}))$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{b:\mathbf{2}}m=\mathsf{rec}_{\mathbf{2}}(b;\mathsf{tt};\mathsf{ff}))$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{b:\mathbf{2}}m=\mathsf{rec}_{\mathbf{2}}(b;\mathsf{tt};\mathsf{ff}))$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big]}{\Big]}{\big]}{]}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big[}{\Big[}{\big[}{[}}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\textstyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}+\mathchoice{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\sum_{b:\mathbf{2}}m=\mathsf{rec}_{\mathbf{2}}(b;\mathsf{tt};\mathsf{ff}))$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\textstyle\textstyle\sum_{b:\mathbf{2}}m=\mathsf{rec}_{\mathbf{2}}(b;\mathsf{tt};\mathsf{ff}))$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{b:\mathbf{2}}m=\mathsf{rec}_{\mathbf{2}}(b;\mathsf{tt};\mathsf{ff}))$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{b:\mathbf{2}}m=\mathsf{rec}_{\mathbf{2}}(b;\mathsf{tt};\mathsf{ff}))$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big]}{\Big]}{\big]}{]}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big[}{\Big[}{\big[}{[}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\textstyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}+\mathchoice{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\sum_{b:\mathbf{2}}m=\mathsf{rec}_{\mathbf{2}}(b;\mathsf{tt};\mathsf{ff}))$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\textstyle\textstyle\sum_{b:\mathbf{2}}m=\mathsf{rec}_{\mathbf{2}}(b;\mathsf{tt};\mathsf{ff}))$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{b:\mathbf{2}}m=\mathsf{rec}_{\mathbf{2}}(b;\mathsf{tt};\mathsf{ff}))$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{b:\mathbf{2}}m=\mathsf{rec}_{\mathbf{2}}(b;\mathsf{tt};\mathsf{ff}))$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big]}{\Big]}{\big]}{]}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big[}{\Big[}{\big[}{[}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\textstyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{e:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})}\mathbf{up}(e)=m$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}+\mathchoice{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\sum_{b:\mathbf{2}}m=\mathsf{rec}_{\mathbf{2}}(b;\mathsf{tt};\mathsf{ff}))$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\textstyle\textstyle\sum_{b:\mathbf{2}}m=\mathsf{rec}_{\mathbf{2}}(b;\mathsf{tt};\mathsf{ff}))$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{b:\mathbf{2}}m=\mathsf{rec}_{\mathbf{2}}(b;\mathsf{tt};\mathsf{ff}))$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big(}{\Big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{b:\mathbf{2}}m=\mathsf{rec}_{\mathbf{2}}(b;\mathsf{tt};\mathsf{ff}))$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big)}{\Big)}{\big)}{)}}}$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big]}{\Big]}{\big]}{]}}}

\displaystyle\alpha_{\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}}

\displaystyle:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\Psi\cong\textstyle\sum_{m:\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool}^{*})}\mathbf{1}\cong\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(z,\mathsf{Bool}(z))

As a result, we obtain
\Phi
along with
\alpha:\Phi\cong\Psi
such that

z:\mathbf{syn}\vdash\alpha^{-1}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle m,\dots$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle m,\dots$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m,\dots$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m,\dots$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}=m
. We may now define
\mathsf{Bool}^{*}
:

\displaystyle\mathfrak{A}_{\mathsf{Bool}^{*}}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{Bool}

\displaystyle\Phi_{\mathsf{Bool}^{*}}

\displaystyle=\Phi

\displaystyle{\uparrow}_{\mathsf{Bool}^{*}}

\displaystyle=\lambda e.\alpha^{-1}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbf{up}(e),\eta(\mathsf{in}_{0}(e,\star))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbf{up}(e),\eta(\mathsf{in}_{0}(e,\star))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbf{up}(e),\eta(\mathsf{in}_{0}(e,\star))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{up}(e),\eta(\mathsf{in}_{0}(e,\star))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}

\displaystyle{\downarrow}_{\mathsf{Bool}^{*}}

\displaystyle=\pi_{0}\circ\alpha

The true and false constants are defined as follows:

\displaystyle\mathsf{true}^{*}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbf{tt},\eta(\mathsf{in}_{1}(0,\star))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbf{tt},\eta(\mathsf{in}_{1}(0,\star))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbf{tt},\eta(\mathsf{in}_{1}(0,\star))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{tt},\eta(\mathsf{in}_{1}(0,\star))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}

\displaystyle\mathsf{false}^{*}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbf{ff},\eta(\mathsf{in}_{1}(1,\star))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbf{ff},\eta(\mathsf{in}_{1}(1,\star))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbf{ff},\eta(\mathsf{in}_{1}(1,\star))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{ff},\eta(\mathsf{in}_{1}(1,\star))$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}

Again, the elimination principle is defined using the induction principle for
\textnormal{$\newmoon$}A
.

\displaystyle\mathsf{if}^{*}

\displaystyle(T_{m},t_{0},t_{1},s=\alpha^{-1}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle b,q$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle b,q$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle b,q$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b,q$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}})=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\mathsf{if}(z,T_{m},t_{0},t_{1},m)&q=\mathsf{in}_{0}(z)\\
{\downarrow}_{T_{m}(s)}\mathbf{if}(T_{m},{\downarrow}_{T_{m}(\mathsf{true}^{*})}t_{0},{\downarrow}_{T_{m}(\mathsf{false}^{*})}t_{1},e)&q=\mathsf{in}_{1}(\mathsf{in}_{0}(e,\star)),\mathbf{up}(e)=b\\
t_{i}&q=\mathsf{in}_{1}(\mathsf{in}_{1}(i,\star)),\mathbf{rec}(i;\mathsf{tt};\mathsf{ff})=b\end{cases}

The boundary conditions and the computation rule follow from computation.
∎

Lemma 7.8
.

The glued model supports a universe. Specifically, this means that the following constants exist:

\displaystyle\mathsf{Uni}^{*}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\displaystyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Uni}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\textstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Uni}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Uni}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{Uni}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}

\displaystyle\mathsf{El}^{*}

\displaystyle:(T:\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni}^{*}))\to\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\displaystyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{El}(T)$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\textstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{El}(T)$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{El}(T)$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{Ty}^{*}_{m}$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z:\mathbf{syn}\mapsto\mathsf{El}(T)$}\mathclose{}{\}}}

Moreover,
\mathsf{Uni}^{*}
is closed under various type-formers:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathsf{Sig}}^{*}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big\{}{\Big\{}{\big\{}{\{}}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big[}{\Big[}{\big[}{[}}\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\sum_{A:\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni}^{*})}\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{El}^{*}(A))\to\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big]}{\Big]}{\big]}{]}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big[}{\Big[}{\big[}{[}}\hbox{$\textstyle\textstyle\sum_{A:\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni}^{*})}\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{El}^{*}(A))\to\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big]}{\Big]}{\big]}{]}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big[}{\Big[}{\big[}{[}}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{A:\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni}^{*})}\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{El}^{*}(A))\to\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big]}{\Big]}{\big]}{]}}}{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\Big[}{\Big[}{\big[}{[}}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\textstyle\sum_{A:\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni}^{*})}\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{El}^{*}(A))\to\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\Big]}{\Big]}{\big]}{]}}}\to\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni}^{*})$}\mathrel{{\mathchoice{\Big|}{\Big|}{\big|}{|}}}\hbox{$\displaystyle \displaystyle\widehat{\mathsf{Prod}}^{*}

It remains to show that the universe is closed under the various codes for dependent sums,
products, etc. For the sake of space, we show only two of these cases: dependent products and
modal types.

Let us fix
t_{0}:(\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni}^{*}))
and

t_{1}:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(\mathsf{El}^{*}(t_{0}))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(\mathsf{El}^{*}(t_{0}))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(\mathsf{El}^{*}(t_{0}))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{n}(\mathsf{El}^{*}(t_{0}))$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni}^{*})

\mathsf{dec}
. Note that by the uniqueness of inverses,

lies over

\mathsf{dec}^{-1}
.

We use this now to define

\displaystyle\mathfrak{A}_{\widehat{\mathsf{Prod}}^{*}(t_{0},t_{1})}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\mathfrak{A}_{t_{0}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\mathfrak{A}_{t_{0}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\mathfrak{A}_{t_{0}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\mathfrak{A}_{t_{0}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\mathrel{\widehat{\to}}\lambda x.\ \mathfrak{A}_{t_{1}({\uparrow}_{t_{0}}(x))}

\displaystyle\Phi_{\widehat{\mathsf{Prod}}^{*}(t_{0},t_{1})}

\displaystyle=\Psi

\displaystyle=\lambda e.\ (\mathsf{dec}^{*}_{\widehat{\mathsf{Prod}}})^{-1}({\uparrow}_{\mathsf{Prod}^{*}(\mathsf{El}^{*}(t_{0}),\mathsf{El}^{*}\circ t_{1})}\mathsf{dec}^{\!\rhd}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle e$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle e$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle e$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle e$}\mathclose{}{)}})

8.
The normalization function

8.1.
Initiality, revisited

As formulated, the cosmos based around the generalized algebraic syntax of
MTT

\mathcal{S}\llbracket-\rrbracket
is
not initial. By
Corollary

3.9
, there is an initial cosmos
\mathcal{I}\llbracket-\rrbracket
but it is a
distinct object and
\mathcal{I}\llbracket m\rrbracket
is not even cocomplete, let alone a presheaf topos.

Despite this,
\mathcal{S}\llbracket-\rrbracket
enjoys a certain distinguished place among
MTT
cosmoi owing to

Theorem

3.7
. In particular, we have the following

(1)

For every context
\Gamma\ \mathsf{cx}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}
, there exists an object
\mathcal{G}\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket:\mathcal{G}\llbracket m\rrbracket
,
together with a canonical isomorphism
\alpha_{\Gamma}:\pi(\mathcal{G}\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket)\cong\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)
.

(2)

For every type
\Gamma\vdash A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}{m}}
, there is a morphism

Lemma 8.1
.

For an arbitrary
X:\mathcal{G}\llbracket m\rrbracket
, we have

\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle(\!|\Theta|\!),X$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle(\!|\Theta|\!),X$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle(\!|\Theta|\!),X$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle(\!|\Theta|\!),X$}\mathclose{}{]}}\cong\mathbf{i}^{*}(X)(\Theta)
.

By the previous discussion, for a telescope
\Theta\ \mathsf{tele}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow\Gamma
, there is also the object

\mathcal{G}\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket
, which lies over
\Gamma
in
\mathcal{G}\llbracket m\rrbracket
. While this object is different
than
(\!|\Theta|\!)
, there is a canonical natural transformation between them.

Lemma 8.2
.

Given
\Theta\ \mathsf{tele}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow\Gamma
, there is a morphism

\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta}:\hbox to70.46pt{\vbox to10pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 12.22221pt\lower-2.5pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {}{}{}{}{{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{}{{
{}{}}}{
{}{}}
{{}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}}{{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{}{{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-12.22221pt}{0.0pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
{
{}{}{}}{{{{}{}{}}}}{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{}{{

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces

\lxSVG@closescope }}}
{

{{{{{}}{

.
Moreover, up to the canonical isomorphism
\mathbf{j}^{*}\mathcal{G}\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\cong\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)
,

\mathbf{j}^{*}\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta}=\mathsf{id}_{\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)}
.

Proof.

By applying
Lemma

8.1
, this proof is equivalent to constructing an
element of
g\in\mathbf{i}^{*}\mathcal{G}\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket(\Theta)
such that the image of
g
under the
canonical map to
\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)$}\mathclose{}{)}}(\Theta)
is the identity. This morphism is constructed by
induction on
\Theta
.

Case:

\frac{}{\mathbf{1}\ \mathsf{tele}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow\mathbf{1}}

In this case these two are isomorphic.

}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-3.00117pt}{0.0pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}

\lxSVG@closescope }}}

{{{{{}}{

{}{}}{}{}{{}{}}}}}{{}}{}{}{}{}{}{}{}{{}}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.92 3.46 L 24.24 3.46}{fill:none} {{}{{}}{}{}{{}}{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{17.7178pt}{2.5pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-dasharray,stroke-dashoffset} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-linecap} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-linejoin} \lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -2.88 3.32 C -2.35 1.33 -1.18 0.39 0 0 C -1.18 -0.39 -2.35 -1.33 -2.88 -3.32}{fill:none} \lxSVG@closescope \lxSVG@closescope }}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces

In this case we choose
\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta.\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}}=\mathcal{G}\llbracket\mu\rrbracket_{!}(\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta_{0}})

correcting by the canonical isomorphisms

Theorem

4.2
and
