construction.

Case:

\frac{\Theta\ \mathsf{tele}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow\Gamma\quad\Gamma.\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}\vdash A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}{n}}}{\Theta.(\mu\mid A)\ \mathsf{tele}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow\Gamma.(\mu\mid A)}

We construct this morphism using the characterization from

Lemma

8.1
. Our induction hypothesis gives us an element

g\in\mathcal{G}\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket_{0}(\Theta)
lying over
\alpha_{\Gamma}^{-1}(\mathsf{id})
, whence

g^{\prime}\in\mathcal{G}\llbracket\Gamma.(\mu\mid A)\rrbracket(\Theta.(\mu\mid A))
lying over
{\uparrow}
using reindexing.

Moreover, we have an element of
\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathcal{S}\llbracket A\rrbracket)(\Theta.(\mu\mid A).\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}})
lying over

\mathbf{v}_{0}
, whence an element of
\mathsf{Tm}^{*}_{m}(\mathcal{G}\llbracket A\rrbracket)(\Theta.(\mu\mid A).\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}})
lying over

\mathbf{v}_{0}
. This gives us an element of
\mathcal{G}\llbracket\Gamma.(\mu\mid A)\rrbracket_{0}(\Theta.(\mu\mid A))
which
lies over
\mathsf{id}={{\uparrow}}.{\mathbf{v}_{0}}
by the universal property of the pullback.
∎

Lemma 8.3
.

Given
\Gamma\vdash M,N:A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}
, if there exists a normal form
u
such that
\Theta\vdash^{\!\!\!\raisebox{0.3014pt}{\tiny$\mathsf{nf}$}}u:A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow M
and

\Theta\vdash^{\!\!\!\raisebox{0.3014pt}{\tiny$\mathsf{nf}$}}u:A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow N
, then
\Gamma\vdash M=N:A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}
.

Lemma 8.4
.

If
\Theta\vdash^{\!\!\!\raisebox{0.3014pt}{\tiny$\mathsf{nf}$}}u{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow A
and
\Theta\vdash^{\!\!\!\raisebox{0.3014pt}{\tiny$\mathsf{nf}$}}u{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow B
, then
\Gamma\vdash A=B{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}{m}}
.

We are now in a position to prove normalization. We note that one of the conditions of the
normalization algorithm (respect for definitional equivalence) is automatic: we have defined the
algorithm to operate only on equivalence classes of terms, so it must always respect definitional
equivalence.

Theorem 8.5
.

There exists a pair of functions
\mathbf{nf}_{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}
and
\mathbf{nfty}_{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}
such that

(1)

If
\Gamma\vdash A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}{m}}
and
\Theta\ \mathsf{tele}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow\Gamma
then
\Theta\vdash^{\!\!\!\raisebox{0.3014pt}{\tiny$\mathsf{nf}$}}\mathbf{nfty}_{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow A
.

(2)

If
\Gamma\vdash M:A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}
and
\Theta\ \mathsf{tele}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow\Gamma
then
\Theta\vdash^{\!\!\!\raisebox{0.3014pt}{\tiny$\mathsf{nf}$}}\mathbf{nf}_{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}:A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow M
.

Proof.

We may package this in the language of
\mathbf{PSh}(\mathsf{Ren}_{m})
. From this viewpoint,

\mathbf{nfty}_{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}
is an element of
(\mathsf{NfTy}_{m})_{0}
fitting into the following triangle:

\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)$}\mathclose{}{)}}
\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)$}\mathclose{}{)}}
(\mathsf{NfTy}_{m})_{0}
\mathchoice{\left\lfloor\mkern-2.0mu{A}\mkern-2.0mu\right\rfloor}{\left\lfloor\mkern-2.0mu{A}\mkern-2.0mu\right\rfloor}{\lfloor A\rfloor}{\lfloor A\rfloor}
\mathsf{NfTy}_{m}

Similarly,
\mathbf{nf}_{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}
is a section to the inclusion
\mathsf{Nf}_{m}
\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

over
\mathchoice{\left\lfloor\mkern-2.0mu{M}\mkern-2.0mu\right\rfloor}{\left\lfloor\mkern-2.0mu{M}\mkern-2.0mu\right\rfloor}{\lfloor M\rfloor}{\lfloor M\rfloor}
.

Observe from the initiality of syntax that for any
\Gamma\vdash A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}{m}}
, there exists

\mathcal{G}\llbracket A\rrbracket :\hbox to64.19pt{\vbox to9pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 14.54861pt\lower-2.0pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {}{}{}{}{{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{}{{

{

, and bi-universality ensures that

\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}\mathcal{G}\llbracket A\rrbracket=A
, up to an isomorphism of contexts. In particular, there exists

A^{\mathbf{nf}}:\hbox to86.6pt{\vbox to10pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 14.54861pt\lower-2.5pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {}{}{}{}{{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{}{{

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-14.54861pt}{0.0pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
{

}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{31.46524pt}{0.0pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}

}
\lxSVG@closescope {{

such that
\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}A^{\mathbf{nf}}=A
.
Unfolding to
\mathbf{PSh}(\mathsf{Ren}_{m})
, we have the following commuting square:

\mathcal{G}\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket_{0}
\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)$}\mathclose{}{)}}
\mathcal{G}\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket
(\mathsf{NfTy}_{m})_{0}
\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathcal{T}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{T}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{T}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
A^{\mathbf{nf}}
\mathsf{NfTy}_{m}
\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\left\lfloor\mkern-2.0mu{A}\mkern-2.0mu\right\rfloor}{\left\lfloor\mkern-2.0mu{A}\mkern-2.0mu\right\rfloor}{\lfloor A\rfloor}{\lfloor A\rfloor}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\left\lfloor\mkern-2.0mu{A}\mkern-2.0mu\right\rfloor}{\left\lfloor\mkern-2.0mu{A}\mkern-2.0mu\right\rfloor}{\lfloor A\rfloor}{\lfloor A\rfloor}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\left\lfloor\mkern-2.0mu{A}\mkern-2.0mu\right\rfloor}{\left\lfloor\mkern-2.0mu{A}\mkern-2.0mu\right\rfloor}{\lfloor A\rfloor}{\lfloor A\rfloor}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\left\lfloor\mkern-2.0mu{A}\mkern-2.0mu\right\rfloor}{\left\lfloor\mkern-2.0mu{A}\mkern-2.0mu\right\rfloor}{\lfloor A\rfloor}{\lfloor A\rfloor}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

By
Lemma

8.2
,
\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta}\in\mathcal{G}\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket_{1}(\Theta)
lies over

\mathsf{id}
. We then define
\mathbf{nfty}_{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}=A^{\mathbf{nf}}(\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta})
, which satisfies the
required properties.

The procedure is identical for
\mathbf{nf}_{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}
. Given
\Gamma\vdash M:A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}
, we use initiality to
obtain the following square:

\mathcal{G}\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket_{0}
\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{y}(\Gamma)$}\mathclose{}{)}}
\mathcal{G}\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket
(\mathsf{Nf}_{m})_{0}
\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_{m}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
M^{\mathbf{nf}}
\mathsf{Nf}_{m}
\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\left\lfloor\mkern-2.0mu{M}\mkern-2.0mu\right\rfloor}{\left\lfloor\mkern-2.0mu{M}\mkern-2.0mu\right\rfloor}{\lfloor M\rfloor}{\lfloor M\rfloor}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\left\lfloor\mkern-2.0mu{M}\mkern-2.0mu\right\rfloor}{\left\lfloor\mkern-2.0mu{M}\mkern-2.0mu\right\rfloor}{\lfloor M\rfloor}{\lfloor M\rfloor}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\left\lfloor\mkern-2.0mu{M}\mkern-2.0mu\right\rfloor}{\left\lfloor\mkern-2.0mu{M}\mkern-2.0mu\right\rfloor}{\lfloor M\rfloor}{\lfloor M\rfloor}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\left\lfloor\mkern-2.0mu{M}\mkern-2.0mu\right\rfloor}{\left\lfloor\mkern-2.0mu{M}\mkern-2.0mu\right\rfloor}{\lfloor M\rfloor}{\lfloor M\rfloor}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

We set
\mathbf{nf}_{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}=M^{\mathbf{nf}}(\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta})
.
∎

Theorem 8.6
.

Given a telescope
\Theta\ \mathsf{tele}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow\Gamma
then the following two facts hold:

(1)

If
\Theta\vdash^{\!\!\!\raisebox{0.3014pt}{\tiny$\mathsf{nf}$}}u:A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow M
, then
\mathbf{nf}_{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}=u
.

(2)

If
\Theta\vdash^{\!\!\!\raisebox{0.3014pt}{\tiny$\mathsf{nf}$}}\tau{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow A
, then
\mathbf{nfty}_{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}=\tau
.

Proof.

In order to prove these results, we show three related facts. Recall that
\mathcal{G}\llbracket-\rrbracket
is the
function sending a piece of syntax to its interpretation in the normalization model. Furthermore,
recall that by
Lemma

8.2

(\!|\Theta|\!)
element

\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta}:\mathcal{G}\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket
.

(1)

If
\Theta\vdash^{\!\!\!\raisebox{0.3014pt}{\tiny$\mathsf{ne}$}}e:A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow M
, then

\mathcal{G}\llbracket M\rrbracket(\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta})={\uparrow}_{\mathcal{G}\llbracket A\rrbracket(\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta})}e

(2)

If
\Theta\vdash^{\!\!\!\raisebox{0.3014pt}{\tiny$\mathsf{nf}$}}u:A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow M
, then

{\uparrow}_{\mathcal{G}\llbracket A\rrbracket(\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta})}\mathcal{G}\llbracket M\rrbracket(\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta})=u
.

(3)

If
\Theta\vdash^{\!\!\!\raisebox{0.3014pt}{\tiny$\mathsf{nf}$}}\tau{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow A
, then
\mathfrak{A}_{\mathcal{G}\llbracket A\rrbracket(\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta})}=\tau
.

Here we have identified a code
u
(resp.
e
) as an
(\!|\Theta|\!)
element of
\mathsf{Nf}_{A}

(resp.
\mathsf{Ne}_{A}
), an abuse justified by unfolding the definition of
\mathsf{Nf}_{A}
into
\mathbf{PSh}(\mathsf{Ren}_{m})
.

Assuming these facts hold, the result immediately follows by unfolding the definition of

\mathbf{nf}_{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}
and
\mathbf{nfty}_{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}
. We prove these facts by mutual induction. All cases
follow from induction except the case of variables, so we show this here.

Case:

\frac{\Theta\ \mathsf{tele}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow\Gamma\quad\Theta=\Theta_{0}.(\mu\mid A)}{\Theta\vdash^{\!\!\!\raisebox{0.3014pt}{\tiny$\mathsf{ne}$}}\mathbf{v}_{0}^{\mathsf{id}_{\mu}}:A\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}\circ{\uparrow}.\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}\circ{\uparrow}.\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}\circ{\uparrow}.\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}\circ{\uparrow}.\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow\mathbf{v}_{0}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\alpha$}\mathclose{}{\}}}$}\mathclose{}{]}}}

(Note that the case for a general
\mathbf{v}_{k}^{\alpha}
follows by straightforward induction on

k
, but is notationally heavy.)

First, we recall that
\mathcal{G}\llbracket\mathbf{v}_{0}\rrbracket
defined by projection from the context. Therefore,

\mathcal{G}\llbracket\mathbf{v}_{0}\rrbracket(\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta})={\uparrow}_{\mathcal{G}\llbracket A\rrbracket(\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta})}\mathbf{v}_{0}^{\mathsf{id}_{\mu}}

by unfolding the construction of
\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta}
in

Lemma

8.2
.

Case:

\frac{\Theta.\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}\vdash^{\!\!\!\raisebox{0.3014pt}{\tiny$\mathsf{nf}$}}\tau{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}n}\rightsquigarrow A}{\Theta\vdash^{\!\!\!\raisebox{0.3014pt}{\tiny$\mathsf{nf}$}}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\tau$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\tau$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\tau$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\tau$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}}

In this case, by unfolding the definitions we have

\displaystyle\mathfrak{A}_{\mathcal{G}\llbracket\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}\rrbracket(\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta.\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}})}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{Mod}_{\mu}(\mathfrak{A}_{A(\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta})})

By induction hypothesis, we have
\mathfrak{A}_{A(\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta})}=\tau
, so the conclusion follows.

Case:

\frac{\Theta.\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}\vdash^{\!\!\!\raisebox{0.3014pt}{\tiny$\mathsf{nf}$}}u:A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}n}\rightsquigarrow M}{\Theta\vdash^{\!\!\!\raisebox{0.3014pt}{\tiny$\mathsf{nf}$}}\mathsf{mod}_{\mu}(u):\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow\mathsf{mod}_{\mu}(M)}

In this case, by unfolding the definitions we have

\displaystyle{\downarrow}_{\mathcal{G}\llbracket\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}\rrbracket(\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta.\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}})}

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}\llbracket\mathsf{mod}_{\mu}(M)\rrbracket(\mathsf{atoms}_{Theta.\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}})

\displaystyle=\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}{{\downarrow}_{\mathcal{G}\llbracket A\rrbracket(\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta})}\mathcal{G}\llbracket M\rrbracket(\mathsf{atoms}_{\Theta})}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}(u)

The last step follows from the induction hypothesis.
∎

Corollary 8.7
.

Each term and type in
MTT
has a unique normal form.

Corollary 8.8
.

The conversion problem in
MTT
is equivalent the conversion problem of normal forms.

Proof.

Theorems

8.5
and
8.3
imply that
\Gamma\vdash M=N:A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}
is
equivalent to
\mathbf{nf}_{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle M,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}=\mathbf{nf}_{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle N,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle N,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle N,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle N,A$}\mathclose{}{)}}
. Similarly,

Theorems

8.5
and
8.4
reduce
\Gamma\vdash A=B{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}{m}}
to

\mathbf{nfty}_{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}=\mathbf{nfty}_{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle B$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle B$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle B$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle B$}\mathclose{}{)}}
. As the proof given is constructively valid, these reductions
are effective.
∎

Corollary 8.9
.

If
\Gamma\vdash\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to B_{0}=\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to B_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}{m}}
, then
\Gamma.\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}\vdash A_{0}=A_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}{m}}
and

\Gamma.(\mu\mid A_{0})\vdash B_{0}=B_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}{m}}
.

Proof.

By inspection on the definition of
\mathbf{nfty}_{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A_{i}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A_{i}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A_{i}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A_{i}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to B_{i}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A_{i}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A_{i}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A_{i}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A_{i}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to B_{i}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A_{i}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A_{i}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A_{i}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A_{i}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to B_{i}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A_{i}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A_{i}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A_{i}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A_{i}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to B_{i}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
, we observe that both are
interpreted using
Lemma

7.5
. Accordingly, we have the following:

\displaystyle\mathbf{nfty}_{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to B_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to B_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to B_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to B_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\displaystyle=u=(\mu\mid\mathbf{nfty}_{\Gamma.\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}})\to\mathbf{nfty}_{\Gamma.(\mu\mid A_{0})}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle B_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle B_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle B_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle B_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\displaystyle\mathbf{nfty}_{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to B_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to B_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to B_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to B_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\displaystyle=v=(\mu\mid\mathbf{nfty}_{\Gamma.\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}})\to\mathbf{nfty}_{\Gamma.(\mu\mid A_{1})}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle B_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle B_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle B_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle B_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

From
\Gamma\vdash\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to B_{0}=\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to B_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}{m}}
and
Corollary

8.8
, we obtain

u=v
. From this, we use inversion to conclude that

\mathbf{nfty}_{\Gamma.\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle A_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}=\mathbf{nfty}_{\Gamma.\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
and

\mathbf{nfty}_{\Gamma.(\mu\mid A_{0})}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle B_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle B_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle B_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle B_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}=\mathbf{nfty}_{\Gamma.(\mu\mid A_{1})}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle B_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle B_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle B_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle B_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
. The result now
follows from
Corollary

8.8
.
∎

Corollary 8.10
.

If modalities and 2-cells enjoy decidable equality, typechecking
MTT
is decidable.

Appendix A
Neutral and normal forms

\framebox{$\displaystyle\Theta\ \mathsf{tele}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow\Gamma$}\\
\frac{}{\mathbf{1}\ \mathsf{tele}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow\mathbf{1}}\frac{\Theta\ \mathsf{tele}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow\Gamma\quad\Gamma.\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}\vdash A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}{n}}}{\Theta.(\mu\mid A)\ \mathsf{tele}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow\Gamma.(\mu\mid A)}\frac{\Theta\ \mathsf{tele}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow\Gamma}{\Theta.\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}\ \mathsf{tele}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}{}\mathop{@}m}\rightsquigarrow\Gamma.\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle n$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle n$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}\mathclose{}{\}}}}\\

Appendix B
A full definition of an internal
MTT
cosmos

We present the full definition of an internal
MTT
cosmos. The fact that these constants
correspond to the exist in e.g. the syntactic
MTT
cosmos follows from unfolding the internal
language this signature is presented in and observing that it is identical to

in cosmoi rich enough to use
MTT
as an internal language.

B.4.
Booleans

\displaystyle\mathsf{Bool}

\displaystyle:\mathsf{Ty}_{m}

\displaystyle\mathsf{true},\mathsf{false}

\displaystyle:\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})

\displaystyle\mathsf{if}

\displaystyle:(A:\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})\to\mathsf{Ty}_{m})\to

\displaystyle\qquad\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(A(\mathsf{true}))\to\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(A(\mathsf{false}))\to

\displaystyle\qquad(b:\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool}))\to\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(A(b))

\displaystyle\_

\displaystyle:(A:\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})\to\mathsf{Ty}_{m})(t:\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(A(\mathsf{true})))(f:\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(A(\mathsf{false})))\to

\displaystyle\qquad(\mathsf{if}(A,t,f,\mathsf{true})=t)\times(\mathsf{if}(A,t,f,\mathsf{false})=f)

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathsf{Bool}}

\displaystyle:\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni})

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathsf{Mod}}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}\to\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni})

\displaystyle\mathsf{dec}_{\widehat{\mathsf{Sig}}}

\displaystyle:(A:\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni}))(B:\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{El}(A))\to\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni}))\to

\displaystyle\qquad\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{El}(\widehat{\mathsf{Sig}}(A,B,)))\cong\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Sig}(\mathsf{El}(A),\mathsf{El}\circ B))

\displaystyle\mathsf{dec}_{\widehat{\mathsf{Prod}}}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}(B:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}_{n}(\mathsf{El}(A))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}_{n}(\mathsf{El}(A))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}_{n}(\mathsf{El}(A))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Tm}_{n}(\mathsf{El}(A))$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni}))\to

\displaystyle\qquad\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{El}(\widehat{\mathsf{Prod}}(A,B)))\cong\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Prod}(\mathsf{El}(A),\mathsf{El}\circ B))

\displaystyle\mathsf{dec}_{\widehat{\mathsf{Bool}}}

\displaystyle:\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{El}(\widehat{\mathsf{Bool}}))\cong\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})

\displaystyle\mathsf{dec}_{\widehat{\mathsf{Mod}}}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{El}(\widehat{\mathsf{Mod}}(A)))\cong\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](\mathsf{El}(A)))

B.7.
Internal and external
MTT
cosmoi coincide

Let us fix a cosmos
F:\mathcal{V}
(see
Definition

3.3
) such that
F
supports a model of

MTT
where types are interpreted (up to equivalence) by families of objects and a
\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid-$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid-$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid-$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid-$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}
is
interpreted by
F(\mu)
. For instance, the syntactic
MTT
cosmos defined by
F(m)=\mathbf{PSh}(\mathsf{Cx}_{m})
.

Theorem B.1
.

The cosmos
F
is an
MTT
cosmos (see
Definition

3.4
) precisely when the
internal language of
F
supports the constants of an internal
MTT
cosmos.

Proof.

First, observe that unfolding the constants
\mathsf{Ty}_{m}
and
\mathsf{Tm}_{m}
into the model in
F
gives an
object
\mathcal{T}_{m}=\llbracket\mathsf{Ty}_{m}\rrbracket
and a family over
\mathcal{T}_{m}
given by
\tau_{m}=\llbracket\mathsf{Tm}_{m}\rrbracket
. We call write
\dot{\mathcal{T}}_{m}
for the domain of
\tau_{m}
. Moreover, this transformation
is a bijection: every family
\tau_{m}
gives rise to a pair of
\mathsf{Ty}_{m}
and
\mathsf{Tm}_{m}
.

Showing that the remain constants induce the structure of an
MTT
cosmos is a standard exercise
in unfolding the interpretation of
MTT
into a presheaf topos. We show only the representative
case of modalities.

First, observe that the constant
\mathsf{Mod}[\mu]:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Ty}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Ty}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Ty}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Ty}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\mathsf{Ty}_{m}
is precisely
determined by a morphism

such that

\tau_{m}\circ m=M\circ F(\mu)(\tau_{n})
.

The final equivalence between the elimination constant
\mathsf{letmod}[\mu;\nu]
and the lifting structure
from
Definition

3.4
is identical similar: unfolding
\mathsf{letmod}[\mu;\nu]
and its
equation amounts to an internal lifting structure.
∎

Appendix C
Neutral and normal forms, internally

We require that all normal and neutral forms become equal to their counterparts in the syntactic
internal
MTT
cosmos (
Appendices

B
and
6
) under the assumption
z:\mathbf{syn}
. We
avoid repeatedly stating this in the specifications of normals and neutrals that follows.

C.1.
Normal types

\displaystyle{\mathbf{Prod}}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{NfTy}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{NfTy}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{NfTy}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{NfTy}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{)}}(B:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\mathsf{V}_{n}(A)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{V}_{n}(A)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{V}_{n}(A)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{V}_{n}(A)$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\mathsf{NfTy}_{m})\to\mathsf{NfTy}_{m}

\displaystyle{\mathbf{Sum}}

\displaystyle:(A:\mathsf{NfTy}_{m})(B:\mathsf{V}_{m}(A)\to\mathsf{NfTy}_{m})\to\mathsf{NfTy}_{m}

\displaystyle\mathbf{Bool}

\displaystyle:\mathsf{NfTy}_{m}

\displaystyle\mathbf{Mod}_{\mu}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\mathsf{NfTy}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{NfTy}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{NfTy}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{NfTy}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}\to\mathsf{NfTy}_{m}

C.2.
Dependent products

\displaystyle\mathbf{lam}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}(B:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Tm}_{n}(z,A(z))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Tm}_{n}(z,A(z))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Tm}_{n}(z,A(z))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Tm}_{n}(z,A(z))$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{m}{z})

\displaystyle{}\to(\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid a:\mathsf{V}_{n}(A)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid a:\mathsf{V}_{n}(A)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid a:\mathsf{V}_{n}(A)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid a:\mathsf{V}_{n}(A)$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(B(a)))

\displaystyle{}\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Prod}(A,B))

\displaystyle\mathbf{app}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}(B:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Tm}_{n}(z,A(z))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Tm}_{n}(z,A(z))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Tm}_{n}(z,A(z))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Tm}_{n}(z,A(z))$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}\mathsf{Ty}_{m})

\displaystyle{}\to\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Prod}(A,B))

\displaystyle{}\to(a:\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(A))

\displaystyle{}\to\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(B(\eta(a)))

C.3.
Dependent sums

\displaystyle\mathbf{pair}

\displaystyle:(A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{m}(z))(B:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(z,A(z))\to\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{m}(z))

\displaystyle{}\to(a:\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(A))\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(B(\eta(a)))

\displaystyle{}\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Sig}(A,B))

\displaystyle\mathbf{proj}_{0}

\displaystyle:(A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{m}(z))(B:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(z,A(z))\to\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{m}(z))

\displaystyle{}\to\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Sig}(A,B))

\displaystyle{}\to\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(A)

\displaystyle\mathbf{proj}_{1}

\displaystyle:(A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{m}(z))(B:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(z,A(z))\to\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{m}(z))

\displaystyle{}\to(p:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Sig}(A,B)))

\displaystyle{}\to\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(B(\eta(\mathbf{proj}_{0}(p))))

C.4.
Booleans

\displaystyle\mathbf{up}

\displaystyle:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})

\displaystyle\mathbf{tt},\mathbf{ff}

\displaystyle:\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})

\displaystyle\mathbf{if}

\displaystyle:(A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(z,\mathsf{Bool}(z))\to\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{m}(z))

\displaystyle{}\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(A(\mathsf{true}))\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(A(\mathsf{false}))\to(b:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool}))\to\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(A(\eta(b)))

C.5.
Modal types

\displaystyle\mathbf{up}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu]{A})\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu]{A})

\displaystyle\mathbf{mod}_{\mu}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(A)$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(A)$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(A)$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(A)$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}\to\mathsf{Nf}_{\mathsf{Mod}[\mu]{A}}

\displaystyle\mathbf{letmod}_{\mu;\nu}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\circ\mu\mid A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\circ\mu\mid A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\circ\mu\mid A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\circ\mu\mid A:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{n}(z)$}\mathclose{}{)}}(B:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\mid a:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(z,\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](z,A(z)))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\mid a:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(z,\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](z,A(z)))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\mid a:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(z,\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](z,A(z)))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\mid a:\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Tm}_{m}(z,\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](z,A(z)))$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\textnormal{$\fullmoon$}_{z}\mathsf{Ty}_{o}(z))

\displaystyle{}\to(\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\circ\nu\mid a:\mathsf{V}_{n}(A)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\circ\nu\mid a:\mathsf{V}_{n}(A)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\circ\nu\mid a:\mathsf{V}_{n}(A)$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\circ\nu\mid a:\mathsf{V}_{n}(A)$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\mathsf{Nf}_{o}(B(\mathsf{m}[\mu](A,\eta(a)))))

\displaystyle{}\to\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\nu\mid a:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](A))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\nu\mid a:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](A))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\nu\mid a:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](A))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu\mid a:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](A))$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\mathsf{Ne}_{o}(B(\eta(a)))

C.6.
Universe á la Tarski

\displaystyle\mathbf{Uni}

\displaystyle:\mathsf{NfTy}_{m}\qquad\mathbf{El}:\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni})\to\mathsf{NfTy}_{m}

\displaystyle\mathbf{up}

\displaystyle:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni})\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni})

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{Prod}}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}(B:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\mathsf{V}_{n}(\mathbf{El}(A))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{V}_{n}(\mathbf{El}(A))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{V}_{n}(\mathbf{El}(A))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{V}_{n}(\mathbf{El}(A))$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni}))\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni})

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{Sum}}

\displaystyle:(A:\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni}))(B:\mathsf{V}_{m}(\mathbf{El}(A))\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni}))\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni})

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{Bool}}

\displaystyle:\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni})

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{Mod}}_{\mu}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}{\mathopen{}{\langle}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{\rangle}}\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni})

\displaystyle\mathbf{dec}^{\!\rhd}_{\widehat{\mathbf{Prod}}}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}(B:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\mathsf{V}_{n}(\mathbf{El}(A))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{V}_{n}(\mathbf{El}(A))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{V}_{n}(\mathbf{El}(A))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{V}_{n}(\mathbf{El}(A))$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni}))

\displaystyle\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}({\mathbf{Prod}}(\mathbf{El}(A),\mathbf{El}(B)))\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{El}(\widehat{\mathbf{Prod}}(A,B)))

\displaystyle\mathbf{dec}^{\!\rhd}_{\widehat{\mathbf{Sum}}}

\displaystyle:(A:\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni}))(B:\mathsf{V}_{m}(\mathbf{El}(A))\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni}))

\displaystyle\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}({\mathbf{Sum}}(\mathbf{El}(A),\mathbf{El}(B)))\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{El}(\widehat{\mathbf{Sum}}(A,B)))

\displaystyle\mathbf{dec}^{\!\lhd}_{\widehat{\mathbf{Prod}}}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}(B:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid\mathsf{V}_{n}(\mathbf{El}(A))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{V}_{n}(\mathbf{El}(A))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{V}_{n}(\mathbf{El}(A))$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid\mathsf{V}_{n}(\mathbf{El}(A))$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni}))

\displaystyle\to\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{El}(\widehat{\mathbf{Prod}}(A,B)))\to\mathsf{Ne}_{m}({\mathbf{Prod}}({\mathbf{El}(A)}{},\mathbf{El}(B)))

\displaystyle\mathbf{dec}^{\!\lhd}_{\widehat{\mathbf{Sum}}}

\displaystyle:(A:\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni}))(B:\mathsf{V}_{m}(\mathbf{El}(A))\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Uni}))

\displaystyle\to\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{El}(\widehat{\mathbf{Sum}}(A,B)))\to\mathsf{Ne}_{m}({\mathbf{Sum}}(\mathbf{El}(A),\mathbf{El}(B)))

\displaystyle\mathbf{dec}^{\!\rhd}_{\widehat{\mathbf{Bool}}}

\displaystyle:\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{El}(\widehat{\mathsf{Bool}}))

\displaystyle\mathbf{dec}^{\!\rhd}_{\widehat{\mathbf{Mod}}_{\mu}}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](A))\to\mathsf{Nf}_{m}(\mathsf{El}(\widehat{\mathsf{Mod}}(A)))

\displaystyle\mathbf{dec}^{\!\lhd}_{\widehat{\mathbf{Bool}}}

\displaystyle:\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{El}(\widehat{\mathsf{Bool}}))\to\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Bool})

\displaystyle\mathbf{dec}^{\!\lhd}_{\widehat{\mathbf{Mod}}_{\mu}}

\displaystyle:\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Nf}_{n}(\mathsf{Uni})$}\mathclose{}{)}}\to\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{El}(\widehat{\mathsf{Mod}}(A)))\to\mathsf{Ne}_{m}(\mathsf{Mod}[\mu](A))

C.7.
Interpreting neutral and normal forms in the glued model

Substantiating these constants in the glued model (see
Theorem

6.1
) relies on unfolding
the constants described above into this model and showing that the expected normal or neutral form
from
Section

5
can be used. These proofs follow
[
Hof99
]
, and again the
main subtlety is showing that binders in constants such as
{\mathbf{Prod}}
are correctly interpreted.

For each normal and neutral form, the requirement that constant lie over the appropriate constant
from the syntactic cosmos forces the “syntactic portion” of each constant. More precisely,
consider again
{\mathbf{Prod}}
and set
I=\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{NfTy}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{NfTy}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{NfTy}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{NfTy}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\times\mathsf{V}_{n}(A)
and

I^{\prime}=\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Ty}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Ty}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Ty}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu\mid A:\mathsf{Ty}_{n}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\times\mathsf{Tm}_{n}(A)
. The definition of
{\mathbf{Prod}}
is a commuting
square of the following shape in
\mathbf{PSh}(\mathsf{Ren}_{m})
:

\llbracket I\rrbracket^{1}
\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\llbracket I^{\prime}\rrbracket$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\llbracket I^{\prime}\rrbracket$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\llbracket I^{\prime}\rrbracket$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\llbracket I^{\prime}\rrbracket$}\mathclose{}{)}}
\llbracket I\rrbracket
\llbracket\mathsf{NfTy}_{m}\rrbracket^{1}
\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\llbracket\mathsf{Ty}_{m}\rrbracket$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\llbracket\mathsf{Ty}_{m}\rrbracket$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\llbracket\mathsf{Ty}_{m}\rrbracket$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\llbracket\mathsf{Ty}_{m}\rrbracket$}\mathclose{}{)}}
\llbracket{\mathbf{Prod}}\rrbracket^{1}
\llbracket\mathsf{NfTy}_{m}\rrbracket
\llbracket{\mathbf{Prod}}\rrbracket^{0}

The bottom map of this square
\llbracket{\mathbf{Prod}}\rrbracket^{0}
, moreover, must be
\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{Prod}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{Prod}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{Prod}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{Prod}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
in order to
ensure that
{\mathbf{Prod}}=\mathsf{Prod}(z)
with
z:U
. It remains to define the top arrow of this
diagram. This is complicated by the difference between the interpretation of dependent products in

\mathcal{G}\llbracket m\rrbracket
and
\mathbf{PSh}(\mathsf{Ren}_{m})
. In particular, while the “downstairs” portion of a dependent
product in
\mathcal{G}\llbracket m\rrbracket
is a dependent product in
\mathbf{PSh}(\mathsf{Cx}_{m})
, the upstairs portion is not a
dependent product in
\mathbf{PSh}(\mathsf{Ren}_{m})
.

In order to define
\llbracket{\mathbf{Prod}}\rrbracket^{1}
, it suffices to fix a morphism

x:\hbox to86.35pt{\vbox to10.64pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 10.8125pt\lower-2.5pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {}{}{}{}{{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{}{{

and define the action of
\llbracket{\mathbf{Prod}}\rrbracket^{1}
on it. First,
observe that we can extend

to the following commuting square:

\mathbf{y}(\Theta)

x
\llbracket I\rrbracket
\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\tilde{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\tilde{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tilde{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tilde{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

By universal property, therefore, we can decompose
x
into a pair:

\mathbf{y}(\Theta.\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\displaystyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\textstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}}{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}\mathclose{}{\}}})

\mathbf{i}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\displaystyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\textstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}{\mathopen{}{[}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}\mathclose{}{]}}^{*}\mathchoice{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\displaystyle\tilde{x}_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\textstyle\tilde{x}_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tilde{x}_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tilde{x}_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

We may now define
{\mathbf{Prod}}(x)=(\mu\mid x_{0})\to x_{1}
. The naturality of this assignment, as well as the
fact that it commutes appropriately, are direct computations.
